#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using index_t = uint32_t;

// Constants
constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: fill
//
// Purpose:
//   Initialize array with random values
//
// Arguments:
//   A: pointer to the array to initialize
//   n: number of elements in the array
//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]
//
// ****************************************************************************
void fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {
    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));
    }
}

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: initRandomMatrix
//
// Purpose:
//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square
//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse
//   row (CSR) format.
//
// Arguments:
//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)
//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;
//                  last element is the index one past the last element
//   n:             number of nonzero elements
//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix
//
// ****************************************************************************
void initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {
    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;

    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned
    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));

    // Seed random number generator
    srand(8675309);

    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned
    bool fillRemaining = false;
    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {
        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;
        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {
            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);
            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;
            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {
                fillRemaining = true;
            }
            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;
            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {
                // Assign (i,j) a value
                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;
                nnzAssigned++;
            }
        }
    }
    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array
    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;
}

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: spmvCpu
//
// Purpose:
//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format
//
// Arguments:
//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix
//   cols: array of column indices for each element
//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;
//                  last element is the index one past the last element
//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication
//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix
//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation
//
// ****************************************************************************
void spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,
             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {
    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {
        double t = 0.0;
        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {
            const auto col = cols[j];
            t += val[j] * vec[col];
        }
        out[i] = t;
    }
}

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: verifyResults
//
// Purpose:
//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution
//
// Arguments:
//   reference: array holding the reference result vector
//   result: array holding the result vector to verify
//   size: number of elements per vector
//
// ****************************************************************************
bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {
    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
        const double ref = reference[i];
        const double res = result[i];
        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
            // For very small values, check absolute error
            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\n", i, ref, res);
                return false;
            }
        } else {
            // Check relative error
            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);
            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, ref, res, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    index_t numRows = 1024;
    index_t sparsity = 10;
    index_t iterations = 10;
    double maxVal = 1.0;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;

    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-m") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }

    // Calculate number of non-zero elements
    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;

    printf("Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %u x %u\n", numRows, numRows);
    printf("Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\n", sparsity);
    printf("Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\n", 
           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));
    printf("Iterations: %u\n", iterations);
    printf("Max value: %.2f\n", maxVal);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");

    // Allocate and initialize data structures
    std::vector<double> h_val(nItems);                  // Non-zero values
    std::vector<index_t> h_cols(nItems);                // Column indices
    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters(numRows + 1);  // Row delimiters
    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);                 // Dense vector
    std::vector<double> h_out(numRows);                 // Output vector

    printf("Initializing data structures...\n");
    fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);
    fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);
    initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);

    // For validation, compute reference solution
    std::vector<double> h_reference;
    if (validate) {
        printf("Computing reference solution...\n");
        h_reference.resize(numRows);
        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), 
                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());
    }

    // Perform SpMV computation
    printf("Computing SpMV...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),
                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_out.data());
    }

    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);

    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    const double gflops = (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    const double avgTime = duration.count() / static_cast<double>(iterations);
    
    printf("Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\n", avgTime);
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(h_out, "OutputVector");
    }

    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        const bool valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);

        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }

    return 0;

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using index_t = uint32_t;

// Constants
constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: fill
//
// Purpose:
//   Initialize array with random values
//
// Arguments:
//   A: pointer to the array to initialize
//   n: number of elements in the array
//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]
//
// ****************************************************************************
void fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {
    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));
    }
}

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: initRandomMatrix
//
// Purpose:
//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square
//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse
//   row (CSR) format.
//
// Arguments:
//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)
//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;
//                  last element is the index one past the last element
//   n:             number of nonzero elements
//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix
//
// ****************************************************************************
void initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {
    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;

    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned
    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));

    // Seed random number generator
    srand(8675309);

    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned
    bool fillRemaining = false;
    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {
        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;
        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {
            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);
            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;
            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {
                fillRemaining = true;
            }
            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;
            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {
                // Assign (i,j) a value
                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;
                nnzAssigned++;
            }
        }
    }
    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array
    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;
}

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: spmvCpu
//
// Purpose:
//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format
//
// Arguments:
//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix
//   cols: array of column indices for each element
//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;
//                  last element is the index one past the last element
//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication
//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix
//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation
//
// ****************************************************************************
void spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,
             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {
    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {
        double t = 0.0;
        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {
            const auto col = cols[j];
            t += val[j] * vec[col];
        }
        out[i] = t;
    }
}

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: verifyResults
//
// Purpose:
//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution
//
// Arguments:
//   reference: array holding the reference result vector
//   result: array holding the result vector to verify
//   size: number of elements per vector
//
// ****************************************************************************
bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {
    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
        const double ref = reference[i];
        const double res = result[i];
        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
            // For very small values, check absolute error
            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\n", i, ref, res);
                return false;
            }
        } else {
            // Check relative error
            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);
            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, ref, res, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    index_t numRows = 1024;
    index_t sparsity = 10;
    index_t iterations = 10;
    double maxVal = 1.0;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;

    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-m") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }

 // Constants

+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
+// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
+// independent work item for every output row.
+__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                           const index_t localRows,
+                           double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localRow >= localRows) {
+        return;
+    }
 
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
+    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
+    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+    }
+    out[localRow] = sum;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
+        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)

+#include <algorithm>
 #include <chrono>
+#include <climits>
 #include <cmath>

 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
 using index_t = uint32_t;
 
 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
+// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
+// independent work item for every output row.
+__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                           const index_t localRows,
+                           double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localRow >= localRows) {
+        return;
+    }
 
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
+    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
+    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+    }
+    out[localRow] = sum;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
+        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+

+#include <algorithm>
 #include <chrono>
+#include <climits>
 #include <cmath>

 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
 using index_t = uint32_t;
 
 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
+// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
+// independent work item for every output row.
+__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                           const index_t localRows,
+                           double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localRow >= localRows) {
+        return;
+    }
 
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
+    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
+    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+    }
+    out[localRow] = sum;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
+        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+

 // Function: fill
 //

 using index_t = uint32_t;
 
 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
+// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
+// independent work item for every output row.
+__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                           const index_t localRows,
+                           double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localRow >= localRows) {
+        return;
+    }
 
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
+    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
+    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+    }
+    out[localRow] = sum;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
+        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+

 using index_t = uint32_t;
 
 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
+// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
+// independent work item for every output row.
+__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                           const index_t localRows,
+                           double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localRow >= localRows) {
+        return;
+    }
 
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
+    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
+    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+    }
+    out[localRow] = sum;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
+        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+

 using index_t = uint32_t;
 
 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
+// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
+// independent work item for every output row.
+__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                           const index_t localRows,
+                           double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localRow >= localRows) {
+        return;
+    }
 
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
+    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
+    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+    }
+    out[localRow] = sum;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
+        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+

+                if (d_cols != nullptr) {
+                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cols));
+                }
+                if (d_out != nullptr) {
+                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_out));
+                }
+                MPI_Comm_free(&localComm);
+                MPI_Finalize();
+                return 1;
+            }
         }
     }
 
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_rowDelimiters));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_vec));
+    if (d_val != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_val));
+    }
+    if (d_cols != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cols));
+    }
+    if (d_out != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_out));
+    }
+    MPI_Comm_free(&localComm);
+    MPI_Finalize();
     return 0;
 }

+find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)
+find_package(CUDAToolkit REQUIRED)

+set_source_files_properties(spmv.cpp PROPERTIES LANGUAGE CUDA)
+
+# Build device code for every architecture supported by the selected CUDA
+# toolkit. This keeps the executable portable across accelerator cluster nodes
+# while providing native SASS whenever the target GPU is among those supported.
+set_target_properties(spmv PROPERTIES
+    CUDA_ARCHITECTURES all
+    CUDA_SEPARABLE_COMPILATION OFF
+)
 
 # Enable optimizations

+target_compile_options(spmv PRIVATE
+    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-O3>
+    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-Xcompiler=-march=native>
+    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-Xcompiler=-Wall,-Wextra>
+)
 
-# Link math library
-target_link_libraries(spmv m)
+# MPI provides inter-node row distribution and OpenMP parallelizes host-side
+# preparation/reference work. CUDA supplies the unconditional SpMV accelerator
+# kernel; no optional CPU fallback is built.
+target_link_libraries(spmv PRIVATE
+    MPI::MPI_CXX
+    OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX
+    CUDA::cudart
+    m

 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
+// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
+// independent work item for every output row.
+__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                           const index_t localRows,
+                           double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localRow >= localRows) {
+        return;
+    }
 
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
+    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
+    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+    }
+    out[localRow] = sum;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
+        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+

+#include <algorithm>

 using index_t = uint32_t;
 
 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
+// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
+// independent work item for every output row.
+__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                           const index_t localRows,
+                           double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localRow >= localRows) {
+        return;
+    }
 
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
+    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
+    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+    }
+    out[localRow] = sum;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
+        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+

codex
The first build succeeded, but its warning exposed an important integration issue: CMake’s C++ OpenMP target supplied the runtime library to the CUDA link, yet did not pass `-fopenmp` through nvcc for this `.cpp`-as-CUDA source. I’m correcting that so the OpenMP regions are active at runtime rather than merely present in the source.
apply patch

 using index_t = uint32_t;
 
 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
+// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
+// independent work item for every output row.
+__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                           const index_t localRows,
+                           double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localRow >= localRows) {
+        return;
+    }
 
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
+    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
+    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+    }
+    out[localRow] = sum;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
+        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+

 using index_t = uint32_t;
 
 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
+// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
+// independent work item for every output row.
+__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                           const index_t localRows,
+                           double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localRow >= localRows) {
+        return;
+    }
 
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
+    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
+    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+    }
+    out[localRow] = sum;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
+        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+

 using index_t = uint32_t;
 
 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
+// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
+// independent work item for every output row.
+__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                           const index_t localRows,
+                           double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localRow >= localRows) {
+        return;
+    }
 
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
+    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
+    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+    }
+    out[localRow] = sum;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
+        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+

 using index_t = uint32_t;
 
 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
+// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
+// independent work item for every output row.
+__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                           const index_t localRows,
+                           double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localRow >= localRows) {
+        return;
+    }
 
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
+    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
+    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+    }
+    out[localRow] = sum;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
+        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+

 using index_t = uint32_t;
 
 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
+// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
+// independent work item for every output row.
+__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                           const index_t localRows,
+                           double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localRow >= localRows) {
+        return;
+    }
 
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
+    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
+    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+    }
+    out[localRow] = sum;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
+        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+

 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
+// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
+// independent work item for every output row.
+__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                           const index_t localRows,
+                           double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localRow >= localRows) {
+        return;
+    }
 
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
+    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
+    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+    }
+    out[localRow] = sum;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
+        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+

The corrected build now compiles without OpenMP warnings, and multi-rank validation passes. I also verified that one-rank and four-rank runs produce identical result statistics and hash, including a case with empty local CSR partitions. I’m doing final edge-case/build checks and reviewing the diff for accidental semantic or cleanup issues.

 using index_t = uint32_t;
 
 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
+// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
+// independent work item for every output row.
+__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                           const index_t localRows,
+                           double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localRow >= localRows) {
+        return;
+    }
 
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
+    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
+    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+    }
+    out[localRow] = sum;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
+        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+

 using index_t = uint32_t;
 
 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
+// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
+// independent work item for every output row.
+__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                           const index_t localRows,
+                           double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localRow >= localRows) {
+        return;
+    }
 
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
+    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
+    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+    }
+    out[localRow] = sum;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
+        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+

    21	constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
    22	constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
    23	
    24	// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
    25	// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
    26	// independent work item for every output row.
    27	__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
    28	                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
    29	                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
    30	                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
    31	                           const index_t localRows,
    32	                           double* __restrict__ out) {
    33	    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    34	    if (localRow >= localRows) {
    35	        return;
    36	    }
    37	
    38	    double sum = 0.0;
    39	    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
    40	    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
    41	    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
    42	        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
    43	    }
    44	    out[localRow] = sum;
    45	}
    46	
    47	[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
    48	                              const char* file, const int line) {
    49	    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
    50	            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
    51	    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
    52	    std::abort();
    53	}
    54	
    55	#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
    56	    do { \
    57	        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
    58	        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
    59	            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
    60	        } \
    61	    } while (false)

    65	//
    66	// Purpose:
    67	//   Initialize array with random values
    68	//
    69	// Arguments:
    70	//   A: pointer to the array to initialize
    71	//   n: number of elements in the array
    72	//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]
    73	//
    74	// ****************************************************************************
    75	void fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {
    76	    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
    77	        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));
    78	    }
    79	}

   333	    // Allocate and initialize data structures

   335	    std::vector<index_t> h_cols;                        // Column indices (rank 0)
   336	    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters(numRows + 1); // Global row delimiters
   337	    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);                 // Dense vector
   338	    std::vector<double> h_out;                          // Output vector (rank 0)
   339

   347	        initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);
   348	    }

   350	    MPI_Bcast(h_vec.data(), static_cast<int>(numRows), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   351	    MPI_Bcast(h_rowDelimiters.data(), static_cast<int>(numRows + 1), MPI_UINT32_T, 0,
   352	              MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   353	
   354	    // Partition rows contiguously so every rank owns a self-contained CSR
   355	    // segment. All ranks compute the partition after the row-pointer broadcast,
   356	    // avoiding an additional metadata exchange.
   357	    std::vector<int> rowCounts(mpiSize);

   359	    std::vector<int> nnzCounts(mpiSize);

   428	    double* d_vec = nullptr;
   429	    double* d_out = nullptr;
   430	    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
   431	
   432	    if (localNnz > 0) {
   433	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_val),
   434	                              static_cast<size_t>(localNnz) * sizeof(double)));
   435	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_cols),
   436	                              static_cast<size_t>(localNnz) * sizeof(index_t)));
   437	    }
   438	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_rowDelimiters),
   439	                          static_cast<size_t>(localRows + 1) * sizeof(index_t)));
   440	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_vec),
   441	                          static_cast<size_t>(numRows) * sizeof(double)));
   442	    if (localRows > 0) {
   443	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_out),
   444	                              static_cast<size_t>(localRows) * sizeof(double)));
   445	    }
   446	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
   447	
   448	    if (localNnz > 0) {
   449	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_val, localVal.data(),
   450	                                   static_cast<size_t>(localNnz) * sizeof(double),
   451	                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
   452	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_cols, localCols.data(),
   453	                                   static_cast<size_t>(localNnz) * sizeof(index_t),
   454	                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
   455	    }
   456	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_rowDelimiters, localRowDelimiters.data(),
   457	                               static_cast<size_t>(localRows + 1) * sizeof(index_t),
   458	                               cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
   459	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_vec, h_vec.data(), static_cast<size_t>(numRows) * sizeof(double),
   460	                               cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
   461	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
   462	
   463	    if (mpiRank == 0) {
   464	        printf("Computing SpMV...\n");
   465	    }
   466	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   467	
   468	    cudaEvent_t startEvent = nullptr;
   469	    cudaEvent_t stopEvent = nullptr;
   470	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&startEvent));
   471	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stopEvent));
   472	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(startEvent, stream));
   473	
   474	    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
   475	        if (localRows > 0) {
   476	            const unsigned gridSize =
   477	                (static_cast<unsigned>(localRows) + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
   478	            spmvKernel<<<gridSize, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
   479	                d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, localRows, d_out);
   480	        }
   481	    }
   482	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   483	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stopEvent, stream));
   484	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stopEvent));
   485	
   486	    float localKernelMilliseconds = 0.0F;
   487	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&localKernelMilliseconds, startEvent, stopEvent));
   488	    float kernelMilliseconds = 0.0F;
   489	    MPI_Reduce(&localKernelMilliseconds, &kernelMilliseconds, 1, MPI_FLOAT, MPI_MAX, 0,
   490	               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   491	
   492	    // The vector is invariant across iterations, so only the final output is
   493	    // copied and gathered. This removes redundant PCIe and interconnect traffic
   494	    // while preserving the externally visible result of the original loop.
   495	    if (iterations > 0 && localRows > 0) {
   496	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(localOut.data(), d_out,
   497	                                   static_cast<size_t>(localRows) * sizeof(double),
   498	                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
   499	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
   500	    }
   501	
   502	    MPI_Gatherv(localOut.empty() ? nullptr : localOut.data(), rowCounts[mpiRank], MPI_DOUBLE,
   503	                mpiRank == 0 ? h_out.data() : nullptr, rowCounts.data(), rowDisplacements.data(),
   504	                MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   505	
   506	    if (mpiRank == 0) {
   507	        const double durationMilliseconds = static_cast<double>(kernelMilliseconds);
   508	        printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", durationMilliseconds);
   509	
   510	        // Calculate performance metrics
   511	        const double seconds = durationMilliseconds / 1000.0;
   512	        const double gflops = seconds > 0.0
   513	                                  ? (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / seconds / 1e9
   514	                                  : 0.0;
   515	        const double avgTime = iterations > 0
   516	                                   ? durationMilliseconds / static_cast<double>(iterations)
   517	                                   : 0.0;
   518	
   519	        printf("Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\n", avgTime);
   520	        printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   521	
   522	        // Print results for external validation
   523	        if (printResults) {
   524	            print_results(h_out, "OutputVector");
   525	        }
   526	
   527	        // Validation
   528	        if (validate) {
   529	            printf("Validating result...\n");
   530	            const bool valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);
   531	
   532	            if (valid) {
   533	                printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   534	            } else {
   535	                printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   536	                CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent));
   537	                CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent));
   538	                CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));
   539	                CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_rowDelimiters));
   540	                CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_vec));
   541	                if (d_val != nullptr) {
   542	                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_val));
   543	                }
   544	                if (d_cols != nullptr) {
   545	                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cols));
   546	                }
   547	                if (d_out != nullptr) {
   548	                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_out));
   549	                }
   550	                MPI_Comm_free(&localComm);
   551	                MPI_Finalize();
   552	                return 1;
   553	            }
   554	        }
   555	    }
   556	
   557	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent));
   558	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent));
   559	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));
   560	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_rowDelimiters));
   561	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_vec));
   562	    if (d_val != nullptr) {
   563	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_val));
   564	    }
   565	    if (d_cols != nullptr) {
   566	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cols));
   567	    }
   568	    if (d_out != nullptr) {
   569	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_out));
   570	    }
   571	    MPI_Comm_free(&localComm);
   572	    MPI_Finalize();
   573	    return 0;
   574	}

 using index_t = uint32_t;
 
 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
+// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
+// independent work item for every output row.
+__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                           const index_t localRows,
+                           double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localRow >= localRows) {
+        return;
+    }
 
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
+    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
+    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+    }
+    out[localRow] = sum;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
+        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+

codex
One cleanup improvement surfaced in the review: validation failure was reported only by rank 0 while other ranks returned success after finalization. I’m making the validation status a collective result so every MPI rank exits consistently, then I’ll perform the final rebuild/check.

 using index_t = uint32_t;
 
 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
+// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
+// independent work item for every output row.
+__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                           const index_t localRows,
+                           double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localRow >= localRows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
+    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
+    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+    }
+    out[localRow] = sum;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
 
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
+        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+

 using index_t = uint32_t;
 
 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
+// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
+// independent work item for every output row.
+__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                           const index_t localRows,
+                           double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localRow >= localRows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
+    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
+    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+    }
+    out[localRow] = sum;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
 
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
+        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+

 using index_t = uint32_t;
 
 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
+// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
+// independent work item for every output row.
+__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                           const index_t localRows,
+                           double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localRow >= localRows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
+    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
+    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+    }
+    out[localRow] = sum;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
 
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
+        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+

 using index_t = uint32_t;
 
 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
+// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
+// independent work item for every output row.
+__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                           const index_t localRows,
+                           double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localRow >= localRows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
+    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
+    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+    }
+    out[localRow] = sum;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
 
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
+        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+

 using index_t = uint32_t;
 
 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
+// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
+// independent work item for every output row.
+__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                           const index_t localRows,
+                           double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localRow >= localRows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
+    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
+    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+    }
+    out[localRow] = sum;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
 
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
+        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+

codex
Implemented the unconditional MPI+OpenMP+CUDA SpMV benchmark.

- MPI distributes CSR row blocks across ranks and maps ranks to local GPUs.
- CUDA performs all timed SpMV iterations.
- OpenMP parallelizes host preparation and validation.
- CMake now requires MPI, OpenMP, and CUDA, while preserving executable name `spmv`.
- No new source files were created.

Modified:

- [spmv.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/spmv_gpt-5.6-luna-xhigh_hybrid_r5/spmv/spmv.cpp)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/spmv_gpt-5.6-luna-xhigh_hybrid_r5/spmv/CMakeLists.txt)

Verified with Release builds and 1/4-rank CUDA runs; validation passed with matching outputs across rank counts.

 using index_t = uint32_t;
 
 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr unsigned CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+// A single CUDA thread owns one row. This preserves the CSR accumulation
+// order of the reference implementation while giving the GPU a regular,
+// independent work item for every output row.
+__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                           const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                           const index_t localRows,
+                           double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const index_t localRow = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localRow >= localRows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[localRow];
+    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[localRow + 1];
+    for (index_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+    }
+    out[localRow] = sum;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, const int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA failure at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
 
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaError = (expression); \
+        if (cudaError != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaError, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+

103,817
Implemented the unconditional MPI+OpenMP+CUDA SpMV benchmark.

- MPI distributes CSR row blocks across ranks and maps ranks to local GPUs.
- CUDA performs all timed SpMV iterations.
- OpenMP parallelizes host preparation and validation.
- CMake now requires MPI, OpenMP, and CUDA, while preserving executable name `spmv`.
- No new source files were created.

Modified:

- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/spmv_gpt-5.6-luna-xhigh_hybrid_r5/spmv/CMakeLists.txt)

Verified with Release builds and 1/4-rank CUDA runs; validation passed with matching outputs across rank counts.
